local v1 = unpack or table.unpack
local v2 = (function()
    local Service
    if not pcall(function()
        Service = game:FindService("HttpService")
    end) or not Service then
        pcall(function()
            Service = game.HttpService
        end)
    end
    if Service and Service.GenerateGUID then
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            return Service:GenerateGUID()
        end)

        if ok then
            ok = type(result) == "string" and #result >= 16
        end

        if ok then
            return "_" .. result
        end
    end

    return "_" .. tostring(math.random(100000, 999999))
end)()
local function v3()
    if v2 then
        return v2 .. tostring(math.random(1000, 9999))
    end

    return "_" .. tostring(math.random(100000, 999999))
end
local v4 = v3()
